"""
machines/mech_interp.py
=======================
MechInterpProbe — Sonden-API für die mechanistische Interpretierbarkeit
der Argon-Maschinen (Spur B, MI-1..MI-4). Rein lesend; Interventionen
(Steering/Ablation) kommen in MI-3 über die Preset-Mechanik von
machine_base hinzu.

Canon-Anker (voll gelesen 2026-10-26):
 - moe_layers.py:56-93   ArgonEntropyAdaptiveRouter.forward
                         -> (top_indices, top_weights, router_entropy);
                         Top-K über scores + expert_bias (k = num_experts_per_tok);
                         last_counts nur im Training belegt — im Eval
                         verlassen, Capture läuft über Forward-Hooks.
 - moe_layers.py:128     gate-Aufruf innerhalb ArgonMoELayer.forward.
 - modeling_argon.py:601-611  Schicht-Schleife: (h, layer_entropy, _) = layer(...).
 - modeling_argon.py:615 h = self.norm(h)   (Final-Norm, Hook-Punkt).
 - modeling_argon.py:733 logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states)
 - modeling_argon.py:735-736 final_softcap — Checkpoint argon_1b_machines:
                         final_logit_softcapping = None  -> Lens-Pfad exakt
                         softmax(lm_head(norm(h))).
 - modeling_argon.py:188-190  ArgonHybridAttention: pattern=6,
                         is_global = (layer_idx % 6 == 5) -> globale
                         MLA-Schichten L5, L11, L17, L23 (auch DecoderLayer
                         :317, hookbar am Layer-Objekt).
 - modeling_argon.py:326-351  ArgonDecoderLayer.forward:
                         h_mid = h_in + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)
                         (:345; attn auf input_layernorm(h_in) :336);
                         h_out = h_mid + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)
                         (:350; MoE-Eingang pre_feedforward_layernorm :348).
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:239  Kanon-Pct-Konvention:
                         ungewichtete Assignment-Bincounts über top_indices,
                         normalisiert counts/counts.sum().clamp(min=1.0)*100.
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:292  Kanon-TV: 0.5 * (a-b).abs().sum().
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:341  Trainings-Hook-Bereich
                         4 <= idx <= 23 (Begründung der Bänder).
 - machine_base.py:224-235  _encode/_gate_hooks-Muster (Vorgänger).

Prinzip (MechanisticCircuitMixMind v1.0, EINHEIT T1): Mittelwert ist kein
Beweis — diese API liefert FELDER (26x8-Matrizen, 26-Vektoren,
Layer-Trajektorien), nie Aggregate ohne Label. Pct-Zeilen sind
Fraction-Konvention (Summe 1.0 je Schicht); Prozent = x100.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterator, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import torch


def canon_tv(a: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Kanon-TV auf Fraction-Skala (train_moe_router_specialization:292):
    0.5 * sum(|a-b|), Wertebereich [0,1]."""
    return 0.5 * float((a - b).abs().sum().item())


def ent_mean(t: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Mittlere Router-Entropie eines Schicht-Slices (float-Ausgabe)."""
    return float(t.detach().float().mean().item()) if t.numel() else 0.0


def layer_bands(num_layers: int) -> Tuple[List[int], List[int]]:
    """Registrierte Bänder der Tiefen-Hypothese: Edge {0..3, L-2, L-1},
    Core {4..L-3} — Training-Hook-Bereich 4 <= idx <= 23
    (train_moe_router_specialization.py:341)."""
    edge = [0, 1, 2, 3, num_layers - 2, num_layers - 1]
    core = list(range(4, num_layers - 2))
    return edge, core


class MechInterpProbe:
    """Rein-lesende Messeinheit: layerwise Routing-Felder + Logit-Lens."""

    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device: Optional[torch.device] = None,
                 max_length: int = 128):
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.device = device if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        self.max_length = int(max_length)
        self.n_layers = len(model.model.layers)
        self.n_experts = int(getattr(model.config, "n_routed_experts", 8))
        self.model.eval()

    # -- Encoding ----------------------------------------------------------
    def _encode(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.tokenizer(
            text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True,
            max_length=self.max_length,
        )["input_ids"].to(self.device)

    # -- Reinheits-Fingerprint (EINHEIT S2) --------------------------------
    def fingerprint(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """expert_bias + gate.weight je Schicht — torch.equal-vergleichbar."""
        return {
            "expert_bias": [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
            "gate_weight": [layer.moe.gate.weight.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
        }

    def fingerprint_equal(self, a: Dict[str, Any], b: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        ok = all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                 zip(a["expert_bias"], b["expert_bias"]))
        return ok and all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                          zip(a["gate_weight"], b["gate_weight"]))

    # -- MI-1: layerwise Routing-Tomographie -------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_routing(self, texts: Sequence[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """26x8 Assignment-Counts (Kanon-Konvention, ungewichtet) +
        mittlere Router-Entropie je Schicht über alle Tokens aller Texte."""
        L, E = self.n_layers, self.n_experts
        counts = torch.zeros((L, E), dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_sum = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_tok = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)

        def _hook(layer_idx: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                top_indices, _, router_ent = out
                counts[layer_idx] += torch.bincount(
                    top_indices.reshape(-1), minlength=E).double().cpu()
                ent_sum[layer_idx] += ent_mean(router_ent) * float(router_ent.numel())
                ent_tok[layer_idx] += float(router_ent.numel())
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].moe.gate.register_forward_hook(
            _hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        try:
            for text in texts:
                ids = self._encode(text)
                _ = self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()

        row_sums = counts.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1.0)
        pct_frac = (counts / row_sums).float()
        ent = (ent_sum / ent_tok.clamp(min=1.0)).float()
        return {
            "counts_26x8": counts.float(),
            "pct_26x8": pct_frac,           # Zeilen summen 1.0 (Kanon: x100 fuer %)
            "pct_percent_26x8": pct_frac * 100.0,
            "entropy_26": ent,
            "n_tokens": int(ent_tok.sum().item()),
        }

    # -- MI-1: Logit-Lens über alle Schichten ------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_logits(
        self, text: str, answer_targets: Optional[Dict[str, Sequence[int]]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Residualstrom-Lens: für jedes h in [Emb, Layer0..Layer{n-1}] der
        offizielle Kopf-Pfad norm -> lm_head (Softcap des Checkpoints
        beachten; argon_1b_machines: None) -> P; Top-1-Trajektorie +
        Antwort-Token-Wahrscheinlichkeit + Gap-TV gegen die fp32-Referenz
        der letzten Schicht. Official-bf16-Logits werden als Anker
        mitgeloggt (anchor_tv, fp32-Lens-vs-bf16-Kopf-Kanal)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        captured: List[torch.Tensor] = []

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.append(_out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.insert(0, o.detach()))
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm
        head = self.model.lm_head

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            # keine dtype-Neuerfindung; anchor_tv deckt die Parität.
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        p_final = _lens(captured[-1])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        official_top1 = int(torch.argmax(official_p).item())

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for idx, h in enumerate(captured):
            p = _lens(h)
            top1 = int(torch.argmax(p).item())
            row: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "layer": -1 if idx == 0 else idx - 1,
                "kind": "emb" if idx == 0 else "layer",
                "top1_id": top1,
                "top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode([top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_prob": float(p[top1].item()),
                "gap_tv": canon_tv(p, p_final),
            }
            if answer_targets:
                traj: Dict[str, float] = {}
                for label, cands in answer_targets.items():
                    vals = [float(p[t].item()) for t in cands if 0 <= t < p.numel()]
                    traj[label] = max(vals) if vals else 0.0
                row["answer_prob"] = traj
            rows.append(row)

        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "p_final_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [int(torch.argmax(p_final).item())], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_id": official_top1,
            "official_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [official_top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_prob": float(official_p[official_top1].item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- MI-2: Sublayer-Zerlegung (Attention vs MoE) ------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def sublayer_logits(self, text: str, answer_target: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Halbschichten-Lens nach modeling_argon.py:326-351:

        h_mid(l) = h_out(l-1) + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)   (:345)
        h_out(l) = h_mid(l) + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)    (:350)

        Capture exakt: forward-hook auf layer.post_attention_layernorm
        (Output = norm(attn_out), bf16) + Layer-Output-Hook (h_out, MI-1-
        Muster) + Emb-Hook (h_out(-1)). h_mid ist die ORIGINAL-bf16-Addition
        :345 — keine Re-Normierung, keine dtype-Neuerfindung (EINHEIT S3).
        P je sublayer über den offiziellen Kopf-Pfad (_lens: norm -> lm_head,
        softmax fp32). dp_attn/dp_moe auf der registrierten target-Koordinate;
        tv_in_mid = canon_tv(P_in, P_mid) als Architektur-Profil-Feld.
        """
        L = self.n_layers
        h_out_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []      # h_out(l) fuer l = -1..L-1
        normed_attn: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * L

        def _norm_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                normed_attn[li] = out.detach()
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, _out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, o.detach(), first=True))
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].post_attention_layernorm
                    .register_forward_hook(_norm_hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        if any(v is None for v in normed_attn):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "sublayer_logits: post_attention_layernorm-Hook fehlte "
                f"({sum(v is None for v in normed_attn)} Schichten) — "
                "Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_out_all) != L + 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits: {len(h_out_all)} statt {L + 1} h_out-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm   # ArgonModel-Submodul (Canon :362;
        head = self.model.lm_head          # :626-634 trägt nur lm_head)

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        target_id = int(answer_target["id"])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        p_final = _lens(h_out_all[-1])
        p_emb = _lens(h_out_all[0])

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for li in range(L):
            h_mid = h_out_all[li] + normed_attn[li]     # exakt :345 (bf16)
            p_in = _lens(h_out_all[li])                 # P(h_out(l-1))
            p_mid = _lens(h_mid)                        # P(h_mid(l))
            p_out = _lens(h_out_all[li + 1])            # P(h_out(l))
            top1_out_id = int(torch.argmax(p_out).item())
            layer = self.model.model.layers[li]
            rows.append({
                "layer": li,
                "is_global": bool(layer.is_global),
                "p_in": float(p_in[target_id].item()),
                "p_mid": float(p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "p_out": float(p_out[target_id].item()),
                "dp_attn": float(p_mid[target_id].item() - p_in[target_id].item()),
                "dp_moe": float(p_out[target_id].item() - p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "tv_in_mid": canon_tv(p_in, p_mid),
                # Registered gap-Lesart (MI-1-Definition, canon_tv): Lens-TV
                # der Schicht-p_out-Verteilung gegen die finale Verteilung
                # p_final (h_out(25)) — Roh-Feld für die Tail-Kontraktion.
                "gap_out_tv": canon_tv(p_out, p_final),
                "top1_out_id": top1_out_id,
                "top1_out_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                    [top1_out_id], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_out_prob": float(p_out[top1_out_id].item()),
            })

        telescope = sum(r["dp_attn"] + r["dp_moe"] for r in rows)
        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "target_id": target_id,
            "target_str": answer_target.get("str", ""),
            "p_emb": float(p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "p_final": float(p_final[target_id].item()),
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "official_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(official_p).item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "telescope_target": telescope,
            "endpoints_target": float(p_final[target_id].item() - p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "telescope_broken": bool(abs(telescope - (p_final[target_id].item()
                                                      - p_emb[target_id].item())) > 1e-6),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- Kristallisations-Finder (EINHEIT T2: Trajektorie statt Mittelwert) --
    def crystallization_start(self, rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[int]:
        """Kleinsten stabilen Layer-Start lesen (Modulfunktion)."""
        return crystallization_start(rows)

    # -- MI-3: Kausale Interventionen (EINHEIT C1–C3) ----------------------
    def _buffers(self, layers: Optional[Sequence[int]]) -> List[int]:
        sel = list(range(self.n_layers)) if layers is None \
            else [int(l) for l in layers]
        for l in sel:
            if not (0 <= l < self.n_layers):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Intervention: Schicht {l} außerhalb [0, {self.n_layers})")
        return sorted(set(sel))

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def steer_band(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                   layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                   steer: float = 50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Hinreichendheits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): expert_bias NUR in den
        gegebenen Schichten auf das Template gesetzt (+steer für experts,
        -steer für den Rest) — SET-Semantik nach machine_base.py:249-256
        (dict-Arm), band-limitiert. Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: experts leer — kein Band-Steering.")
        if not steer > 0:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: steer muss > 0 sein (Amplitude).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_band_steer(layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data,
                                 experts, steer)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "steer": float(steer),
                   "mode": "set"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"steer_band: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def knockout_experts(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                         layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                         penalty: float = -50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Notwendigkeits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): die gegebenen Experten in den
        gegebenen Schichten ADDITIV stummschalten (g[e] += penalty auf dem
        GELERNTEN Original-Bias), die übrigen Experten tragen lernende
        Bias weiter (MI-3-Audits). Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: experts leer.")
        if penalty >= 0:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: penalty muss < 0 sein "
                             "(Stummschaltung).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_expert_knockout(
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, experts, penalty)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "penalty": float(penalty),
                   "mode": "additive"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"knockout_experts: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @torch.no_grad()
    def digit_mass(self, text: str, tau: float = 0.01) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """M_digit = Σ_d P_full(id(d)) über die voll-Vokabular-Softmax
        (SP3-Folge-Canon, machine_base.py:281-322). Resolution wie der
        Canon: je Ziffer ein einziges Token mit decode.lstrip == str(d);
        sonst ValueError (kein stiller Fallback). Meldung 'unknown' als
        Feld, nicht als Rückgabe-Abkürzung (M_raw < tau -> unknown=True)."""
        p = self.last_probs(text)
        digit_ids: List[int] = []
        for d in range(10):
            ids = self.tokenizer.encode(str(d), add_special_tokens=False)
            token_id = int(ids[0]) if len(ids) == 1 else -1
            decoded = (self.tokenizer.decode([token_id], skip_special_tokens=True)
                       if token_id >= 0 else "?")
            if token_id < 0 or decoded.lstrip(" ") != str(d):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"digit_mass: Ziffer '{d}' löst nicht als ein-Ziffern-"
                    f"Token auf (decode='{decoded}') — kein stiller Fallback.")
            digit_ids.append(token_id)
        probs = p.index_select(
            0, torch.tensor(digit_ids, device=p.device))
        mass = float(probs.sum().item())
        top_d = int(torch.argmax(probs).item()) if mass > 0.0 else None
        return {"M_digit": mass, "unknown": mass < tau,
                "digit_probs": {str(d): float(probs[i].item())
                                for i, d in enumerate(range(10))},
                "top_digit": top_d}

    @torch.no_grad()
    def last_probs(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Offizielle End-Verteilung P(logits[last], softmax) — der Kopf-Pfad
        des Modells (Canon :615/:733), fp32-Softmax."""
        ids = self._encode(text)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0, -1].detach()
        return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def token_probs(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """[T, V] fp32-Softmax je Token-Position (teacher-forced; offizieller
        Kopf-Pfad) — Basis für SEQ-TV / CE-Messungen über die Sequenz."""
        ids = self._encode(text)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0].detach()
        return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def tokenwise_routing(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Tokenweise Routing-Trajektorie (EINHEIT T2, MI-4-Instrument):
        Forward-Hooks auf allen 26 Gates; pct[T][26][8] trägt die
        normalisierten top_weights (inkl. capacity_gate und 0.15-Dämpfung,
        exakt canon moe_layers.py:80-92), ent[T][26] die canon
        router_entropy je Token und Schicht. Assembly in assemble_pct (R2:
        pilot-lauffähig ohne Modell)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        collected: List[Tuple[int, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = []

        def _gate_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                top_indices, top_weights, router_ent = out
                collected.append((li, top_indices.detach(),
                                  top_weights.detach(),
                                  router_ent.detach().float()))
            return hook_fn

        handles = [layer.moe.gate.register_forward_hook(_gate_hook(li))
                   for li, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers)]
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
        if len(collected) != L:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"tokenwise_routing: {len(collected)} statt {L} Gate-Hook-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        return assemble_pct(collected, self.n_experts, L)

    # -- MI-5: Dense-arm (gemma-3-1b-it) ------------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def sublayer_logits_dense(
        self, text: str, answer_target: Dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Dense-Sublayer-Lens für gemma-3-1b-it (MI-5-Instrument; die
        KONDITION der Plansektion „dense-arm" ist erfüllt: Struktur-Scout
        scratches/scout_gemma_structure.py, exit 0 — Gemma3ForCausalLM,
        26 Schichten, input_layernorm/post_attention_layernorm vorhanden,
        Hook-Formen (1,T,1152) bf16, kein Softcapping).

        Konstruktion exakt am Gemma3-Decoder-Forward (transformers
        models/gemma3/modeling_gemma3.py:415-434):
            h_mid(l) = h_in(l) + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)    (:426)
            h_out(l) = h_mid(l) + post_ffn_norm(mlp(pre_ffn_norm(h_mid)))
        — dieselbe residual-Familie wie der Argon-Canon (modeling_argon.py:
        345), weshalb dp_attn identisch konstruierbar ist. dp_mlp ist das
        Rest-Delta p_out − p_mid und deckt die komplette MLP-Zweig-Normung.
        Capture: Layer-Pre-Hook (h_in) + post_attention_layernorm-Hook
        (normiertes attn, exakt MI-2-Muster) + Layer-Output-Hook (h_out,
        MI-1-Muster) + Emb-Hook. P je sublayer über den offiziellen Kopf-
        Pfad norm -> lm_head; Softmax auf fp32-Streu (EINHEIT S3)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        h_out_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []      # h_out(l) fuer l = -1..L-1
        h_in_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []       # h_in(l) l = 0..L-1 (pre-hook)
        normed_attn: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * L

        def _norm_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                normed_attn[li] = out.detach()
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_pre_hook(
            lambda _m, args: h_in_all.append(args[0].detach()))
            for idx in range(L)]
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, _out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        handles.append(self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, o.detach(), first=True)))
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].post_attention_layernorm
                    .register_forward_hook(_norm_hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()

        if any(v is None for v in normed_attn):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "sublayer_logits_dense: post_attention_layernorm-Hook fehlte "
                f"({sum(v is None for v in normed_attn)} Schichten) — "
                "Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_out_all) != L + 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits_dense: {len(h_out_all)} statt {L + 1} "
                "h_out-Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_in_all) != L:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits_dense: {len(h_in_all)} statt {L} h_in-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm   # Gemma3RMSNorm (Scout-Fakt)
        head = self.model.lm_head

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Offizieller Kopf-Pfad der Gemma3-Kette (kein Softcapping —
            # config-Werte None, Scout-Fakt), Softmax über fp32-Streu:
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        target_id = int(answer_target["id"])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        p_final = _lens(h_out_all[-1])
        p_emb = _lens(h_out_all[0])

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for li in range(L):
            h_mid = h_in_all[li] + normed_attn[li]      # exakt gemma3:426
            p_in = _lens(h_in_all[li])                  # P(h_in(l))
            p_mid = _lens(h_mid)                        # P(h_mid(l))
            p_out = _lens(h_out_all[li + 1])            # P(h_out(l))
            top1_out_id = int(torch.argmax(p_out).item())
            rows.append({
                "layer": li,
                "p_in": float(p_in[target_id].item()),
                "p_mid": float(p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "p_out": float(p_out[target_id].item()),
                "dp_attn": float(p_mid[target_id].item() - p_in[target_id].item()),
                "dp_mlp": float(p_out[target_id].item() - p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "tv_in_mid": canon_tv(p_in, p_mid),
                "gap_out_tv": canon_tv(p_out, p_final),
                "top1_out_id": top1_out_id,
                "top1_out_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                    [top1_out_id], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_out_prob": float(p_out[top1_out_id].item()),
            })

        telescope = sum(r["dp_attn"] + r["dp_mlp"] for r in rows)
        endpoints = float(p_final[target_id].item() - p_emb[target_id].item())
        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "target_id": target_id,
            "target_str": answer_target.get("str", ""),
            "p_emb": float(p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "p_final": float(p_final[target_id].item()),
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "official_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(official_p).item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "telescope_target": telescope,
            "endpoints_target": endpoints,
            "telescope_broken": bool(abs(telescope - endpoints) > 1e-6),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }


CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL = 2  # Registrierung: stabiler TAIL >= 2 Schichten gilt als Fenster


def crystallization_start(rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                          min_tail: int = CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL) -> Optional[int]:
    """Kleinster Layer-Index, ab dem top1 stabil bis zum ENDE bleibt und der
    stabile TAIL mindestens ``min_tail`` Schichten spannt; None, wenn kein
    stabiler Start existiert (Oszillation, Via-Negativa-Datum)."""
    layer_rows = [r for r in rows if r["kind"] == "layer"]
    if not layer_rows:
        return None
    final_top1 = layer_rows[-1]["top1_id"]
    for start_idx in range(len(layer_rows)):
        span = len(layer_rows) - start_idx
        if span < min_tail:
            break
        if all(r["top1_id"] == final_top1 for r in layer_rows[start_idx:]):
            return layer_rows[start_idx]["layer"]
    return None


def _out_layer(o: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Decoder-Layer-Output (h, layer_entropy, present_kv) -> Residual h."""
    return o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o.detach()


def apply_band_steer(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                     steer: float) -> None:
    """Band-Steering auf EINE Bias-Zeile: SET-Semantik nach dem registrierten
    dict-Arm von machine_base.steer (fill_(-steer) + g[e]=+steer) — das
    Template ueberschreibt den gelernten Bias fuer die Dauer des Blocks;
    Restaurierung macht der ContextManager (C3, copy_-Muster)."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        buffer.fill_(-float(steer))
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_band_steer: Experte {e} außerhalb [0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] = float(steer)


def apply_expert_knockout(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                          penalty: float) -> None:
    """Experten-Knockout auf EINE Bias-Zeile: ADDITIV auf den gelernten
    Original-Bias (die Spezialisten werden stummgeschaltet, alle übrigen
    tragen lernende Bias weiter — Notwendigkeits-Lesart des MI-3-Audits).
    In-place; Restaurierung macht der ContextManager."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_expert_knockout: Experte {e} außerhalb "
                    f"[0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] += float(penalty)


def assemble_pct(collected: Sequence[Tuple[int, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor,
                                       torch.Tensor]],
                 n_experts: int, n_layers: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Canon-Assembly der tokenweisen Routing-Captures (moe_layers.py:
    top_weights sind normalisiert + ggf. 0.15-gedämpft + capacity_gate-
    skaliert, sonst 0): pct[T][L][E], ent[T][L]. Reine Tensor-Mathe — vom
    CPU-Piloten ohne Modell ausführbar (R2)."""
    if not collected:
        raise ValueError("assemble_pct: leere Capture-Liste.")
    ordered = sorted(collected, key=lambda row: row[0])
    if [row[0] for row in ordered] != list(range(n_layers)):
        raise ValueError(
            "assemble_pct: Schichtindizes unvollständig oder außerhalb der "
            f"Reihenfolge ({[row[0] for row in ordered]}).")
    ent_first = ordered[0][3]
    T = int(ent_first.shape[0])
    pct = [[[0.0] * n_experts for _ in range(n_layers)] for _ in range(T)]
    ent: List[List[float]] = [[0.0] * n_layers for _ in range(T)]
    for li, top_indices, top_weights, router_ent in ordered:
        if int(router_ent.shape[0]) != T:
            raise ValueError(
                "assemble_pct: inkonsistente Token-Zahl über Schichten "
                f"({int(router_ent.shape[0])} vs {T}).")
        for t in range(T):
            ent[t][li] = float(router_ent[t].item())
            for k in range(int(top_weights.shape[1])):
                e_idx = int(top_indices[t, k].item())
                pct[t][li][e_idx] += float(top_weights[t, k].item())
    return {"pct": pct, "ent": ent, "n_tokens": T, "n_layers": n_layers,
            "n_experts": n_experts}


def _store_layer(store: List[torch.Tensor], h: torch.Tensor,
                 first: bool = False) -> None:
    """Geordnete h_out-Capture-Einheit (EINHEIT S2): Emb-Hook schiebt an
    index 0 (h_out(-1)); Layer-Hooks feuern je Layer in Forward-Reihenfolge
    und haengen an — die Liste ist dann [-1, 0, 1, ..., L-1]."""
    if first:
        store.insert(0, h)
    else:
        store.append(h)


# == MI-5: Cross-Model-Zugänge (gemma-3-1b-it + Argon-Schwestern) ==========

def is_moe_model(model: Any) -> bool:
    """MI-5-Strukturwächter (Guards statt Simulation, Via-Negativa): True,
    wenn Decoder-Schicht 0 ein .moe trägt (Argon-Linie: 26 × {gate,
    experts_per_tok 2}); False auf DENSE-Modellen (gemma-3-1b-it — kein
    .moe, Scout-Fakt). Was strukturell fehlt, wird nicht simuliert; der
    Smoke bucht solche Felder OFFEN/strukturell."""
    try:
        layers = model.model.layers
        return bool(len(layers) > 0 and hasattr(layers[0], "moe"))
    except AttributeError:
        return False


def load_any_checkpoint(path: str, device: Optional[str] = None,
                        ) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
    """MI-5 Voll-Checkpoint-Lader im Canon-Muster von verify_machines.py:
    339-356 (ArgonConfig(**cfg) -> ArgonForConditionalGeneration bf16 ->
    load_state_dict -> AutoTokenizer, pad_token-Canon), OHNE router_diff —
    die Schwester-Checkpoints tragen den kompletten Zustand (single
    model.safetensors je 4.8 G). STRICT-Load (EINHEIT S1): fehlende oder
    unerwartete Keys → RuntimeError, kein stiller Fallback. device-Canon:
    'cuda' wenn verfügbar, sonst 'cpu'."""
    import json as _json
    import os as _os
    from safetensors.torch import load_file as _load_file
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer as _AutoTokenizer
    from modeling_argon import ArgonConfig, ArgonForConditionalGeneration

    dev = device or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    with open(_os.path.join(path, "config.json")) as handle:
        cfg_dict = _json.load(handle)
    config = ArgonConfig(**cfg_dict)
    model = ArgonForConditionalGeneration(config).to(dtype=torch.bfloat16,
                                                     device=dev)
    state = _load_file(_os.path.join(path, "model.safetensors"))
    model.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
    tokenizer = _AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(path)
    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
    return model, tokenizer

# Registrierte Chimären (User-Audit MI-4, verbatim) und t_switch-Praefixe:
CHIMERA_PROSE_CODE = ("Der warme Sommerwind wehte über den See, während "
                      "das Programm lief: def quicksort(arr): "
                      "return [x for x in arr]")
CHIMERA_PROSE_MATH = ("Oma erzählte im alten Garten ihre Geschichte, "
                      "doch plötzlich galt: Für alle n in N gilt "
                      "sum_{k=1}^n k = n(n+1)/2.")
PREFIX_PROSE_CODE = ("Der warme Sommerwind wehte über den See, "
                     "während das Programm lief: ")
PREFIX_PROSE_MATH = ("Oma erzählte im alten Garten ihre Geschichte, "
                     "doch plötzlich galt: ")

KO_SET_PROSE: Tuple[int, ...] = (3, 7)      # Prosa-Träger (MI-1/MI-3-Canon)
KO_SET_CODE: Tuple[int, ...] = (0, 1, 4)    # Code-Träger (MI-1/MI-3-Canon)
KO_PENALTY = -50.0           # MI-3-Canon (knockout_experts-ADDITIV-Arm)
STEER_AMPLITUDE = 50.0       # MI-3-Canon (steer_band-SET-Arm)

N_LAYERS = 26
N_EXPERTS = 8
_EDGE_LIST, _CORE_LIST = layer_bands(N_LAYERS)
BAND_CORE: Tuple[int, ...] = tuple(_CORE_LIST)          # 4..23 (Kanon)

# MI-1-Subset-Canon (je Text[0] der Domänen):
DOMAINS: Dict[str, str] = {
    "code":
        "def quicksort(items):\n    if len(items) <= 1:\n        return items\n"
        "    pivot = items[0]\n    return quicksort([x for x in items[1:] if x < pivot])"
        " + [pivot] + quicksort([x for x in items[1:] if x >= pivot])",
    "math_logic":
        "For all natural numbers n, the sum 1 + 2 + ... + n equals n(n+1)/2.",
    "prose":
        "Der warme Sommerwind trug in der Abenddämmerung den Duft von Kiefern "
        "über den stillen Bergsee.",
    "goedel":
        "Die Gödelnummer eines Formelausdrucks entsteht aus Primfaktoren: "
        "2^1 · 3^2 · 5^1 kodiert die Zeichenfolge (x).",
    "turing":
        "Eine Turingmaschine liest das Symbol, schreibt das neue Symbol, "
        "bewegt den Kopf nach links oder rechts und wechselt den Zustand.",
    "wittgenstein":
        "Ein Wort hat Bedeutung im Gebrauch, nicht im Wörterbuch — das "
        "Sprachspiel gibt der Zeichenfolge ihre Rolle.",
}
MI5_SUBSET: Tuple[str, ...] = tuple(DOMAINS.values())
V5_TEXT_PROSE = DOMAINS["prose"]   # Bergsee
V5_TEXT_CODE = DOMAINS["code"]     # quicksort

# Registrierte Verdikt-Schwellen (PREREG, siehe Docstring):
FORMAT_TOKENS = ("", "\n", "\n\n", " ", ".", ",", ":", ";", "!", "?")
TV_FLOOR = 0.01      # V1
V3_P1_FLOOR = 0.05   # V3b
V3_M_FLOOR = 0.10    # V3c
V4_MIN_DEFINED = 4   # V4 (>= 4/6)
V5_GAP_FLOOR = 0.01  # V5
V6_SHARE_FLOOR = 0.5 # V6


def common_prefix_len(a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
    """Gemeiname Token-Praefixlaenge (t_switch-Marker, MI-4-Kanon)."""
    n = 0
    for x, y in zip(a, b):
        if x != y:
            break
        n += 1
    return n


def resolve_token_ids_exact(tokenizer, target: str,
                            candidates: Tuple[str, ...]) -> List[int]:
    """MI-2-P4-Auflösung (Oberflächen-Exakt): len(encoded) == 1 UND
    decode([id]) == target EXAKT (kein lstrip); Mehr-Token-Kandidaten
    abgewiesen, bevor ein Vertreter entsteht."""
    ids: List[int] = []
    for cand in candidates:
        encoded = tokenizer.encode(cand, add_special_tokens=False)
        if len(encoded) != 1:
            continue
        decoded_first = tokenizer.decode([encoded[0]], skip_special_tokens=True)
        if decoded_first == target:
            ids.append(int(encoded[0]))
    return ids


def mean_value(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    return sum(xs) / len(xs) if xs else float("nan")


def layer_mean_tv(pct_s: torch.Tensor, pct_b: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """V1-Distanz eines Texts: (1/26) Σ_li canon_tv(pct_S[li], pct_B[li])."""
    if pct_s.shape != pct_b.shape or pct_s.shape[-1] != N_EXPERTS:
        raise ValueError("layer_mean_tv: Form-Bruch — kein stiller Fallback.")
    return mean_value([canon_tv(pct_s[li], pct_b[li])
                       for li in range(pct_s.shape[0])])


def gate_value(ent: float) -> float:
    """Capacity-Gate canon (moe_layers.py:85-88)."""
    return min(max(ent / math.log(N_EXPERTS), 0.25), 1.0)


# -- CPU-Pilot (PILOT-Labels, promovieren keine Verdikte — R2) ---------------

def pilot_canon_tv_axioms() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    a = torch.tensor([0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1])
    b = torch.tensor([0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    tv_same = float(canon_tv(a, a))
    tv_sym = canon_tv(a, b) == canon_tv(b, a)
    tv_perm = canon_tv(a, torch.tensor([0.3, 0.4, 0.1, 0.2])) > 0.0
    tv_in_range = 0.0 <= canon_tv(a, b) <= 1.0
    ok = (tv_same < 1e-9) and bool(tv_sym) and bool(tv_perm) and bool(tv_in_range)
    return ("pilot_canon_tv_axioms", ok,
            f"tv_same={tv_same:.2e} sym={bool(tv_sym)} perm={bool(tv_perm)} "
            f"range={bool(tv_in_range)}")


def pilot_crystal_finder() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    def rows_of(seq: List[int]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return [{"kind": "layer", "layer": i, "top1_id": t}
                for i, t in enumerate(seq)]

    star_const = crystallization_start(rows_of([7, 7, 5, 9] + [42] * 20))
    star_late = crystallization_start(rows_of([7] * 10 + [9] * 24))
    star_osc = crystallization_start(rows_of([7, 9] * 13))
    ok = (star_const == 4 and star_late == 10 and star_osc is None)
    return ("pilot_crystal_finder", ok,
            f"const={star_const} late={star_late} osc={star_osc}")


def pilot_layer_bands_26() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    edge, core = layer_bands(N_LAYERS)
    union = sorted(edge + core)
    ok = (union == list(range(N_LAYERS)) and len(union) == len(set(union)))
    return ("pilot_layer_bands_26", ok,
            f"edge={edge} core_first={core[0]} core_last={core[-1]}")


def pilot_gate_clamp() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    ln8 = math.log(N_EXPERTS)
    ok = (abs(gate_value(0.15 * ln8) - 0.25) < 1e-9
          and abs(gate_value(0.5 * ln8) - 0.5) < 1e-9
          and abs(gate_value(9.0 * ln8) - 1.0) < 1e-9)
    return ("pilot_gate_clamp", ok,
            "floor 0.15·ln8, linear 0.5·ln8, cap 9.0·ln8 (moe_layers.py:85-88)")


def pilot_moe_guard() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    class _LayerWithMoe:
        moe = object()

    class _MoeStub:
        model = type("_M", (), {"layers": [_LayerWithMoe()]})()

    class _DenseStub:
        model = type("_M", (), {"layers": [object()]})()

    class _BrokenStub:
        pass

    ok = (is_moe_model(_MoeStub) is True
          and is_moe_model(_DenseStub) is False
          and is_moe_model(_BrokenStub) is False)
    return ("pilot_moe_guard", ok, "moe->True dense->False broken->False")


def pilot_resolver_contract() -> Tuple[str, bool, str]:
    class _Tok:
        def encode(self, text: str, add_special_tokens: bool = False):
            return {" Paris": [9079], "Paris": [500, 9079]}[text]

        def decode(self, ids, skip_special_tokens: bool = True):
            return {9079: " Paris"}[ids[0]]

    got = resolve_token_ids_exact(_Tok(), " Paris", (" Paris", "Paris"))
    empty = resolve_token_ids_exact(_Tok(), " Span", (" Paris",))
    ok = (got == [9079] and empty == [])
    return ("pilot_resolver_contract", ok,
            f"unique={got} mehr_token_verworfen leere_treffer={empty}")


PILOTS: List = [
    pilot_canon_tv_axioms, pilot_crystal_finder, pilot_layer_bands_26,
    pilot_gate_clamp, pilot_moe_guard, pilot_resolver_contract,
]


# -- Batterie-Felder ---------------------------------------------------------

def bias_std_26(model) -> List[float]:
    """Datum je argon-Variante: std des learned expert_bias je Schicht."""
    return [float(layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.std().item())
            for layer in model.model.layers]


def sp3_fields(probe: MechInterpProbe) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """SP3-Argmax am GENERIC_PROMPT (raw) + Paris-Recall-Felder."""
    p = probe.last_probs(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    top1 = int(p.argmax().item())
    m_gen = probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    p_paris = probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT)
    paris_top1 = int(p_paris.argmax().item())
    return {
        "generic_top1_id": top1,
        "generic_top1_str": probe.tokenizer.decode(
            [top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
        "p_digit1_generic": m_gen["digit_probs"]["1"],
        "M_digit_generic": m_gen["M_digit"],
        "M_unknown": m_gen["unknown"],
        "paris_top1_id": paris_top1,
        "paris_top1_str": probe.tokenizer.decode(
            [paris_top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
    }


def m_digit_series(probe: MechInterpProbe) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """[Datums-Zusatz] M_digit-Serie unter MI-3-Canon-Bias-Bedingungen."""
    raw = float(probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)["M_digit"])
    series: Dict[str, Any] = {"raw": raw}
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_SET_PROSE, layers=list(BAND_CORE),
                                penalty=KO_PENALTY):
        series["ko_prose_core"] = float(
            probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)["M_digit"])
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_SET_CODE, layers=list(BAND_CORE),
                                penalty=KO_PENALTY):
        series["ko_code_core"] = float(
            probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)["M_digit"])
    with probe.steer_band(KO_SET_PROSE, None, steer=STEER_AMPLITUDE):
        series["steer_full_prose"] = float(
            probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)["M_digit"])
    return series


def chimera_tokenwise(probe: MechInterpProbe, chimera: str,
                      prefix: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """[Datums-Zusatz] Chimären-Felder je argon-Variante (t_switch per IDs)."""
    ids_full = probe._encode(chimera)[0].tolist()
    ids_pre = probe._encode(prefix)[0].tolist()
    t_switch = common_prefix_len(ids_full, ids_pre)
    rt = probe.tokenwise_routing(chimera)
    T = rt["n_tokens"]
    if not (1 <= t_switch < T):
        raise RuntimeError(f"chimera_tokenwise: t_switch={t_switch}/{T}")
    return {"text": chimera,
            "prefix": prefix,
            "t_switch": t_switch,
            "n_tokens": T,
            "pct": rt["pct"],
            "ent": rt["ent"]}


def ent_l11_series(probe: MechInterpProbe) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """V5-Feld: mean router entropy ℓ11 (tokenweise, Mi-4-Kanon) auf
    Bergsee (prose) und quicksort (code)."""
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name, text in (("prose", V5_TEXT_PROSE), ("code", V5_TEXT_CODE)):
        rt = probe.tokenwise_routing(text)
        ent = rt["ent"]                       # Liste [T][26] (assemble_canon)
        H = [ent[t][11] for t in range(rt["n_tokens"])]
        h_mean = mean_value(H)
        out[name] = {"H_mean_l11": float(h_mean),
                     "H_min_l11": float(min(H)),
                     "H_max_l11": float(max(H)),
                     "gate_mean_l11": float(gate_value(h_mean))}
    return out


def subset_rows(probe: MechInterpProbe) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Logit-Lens-Trajektorien je Subset-Text (modell-agnostisch)."""
    return {name: probe.layerwise_logits(text)
            for name, text in DOMAINS.items()}


# -- Läufe je Modell ---------------------------------------------------------

def gpu_determinism() -> None:
    torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=False)
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False


def empty_cache() -> None:
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


def load_model(model_name: str) -> MechInterpProbe:
    print(f"[MI-5] lade {model_name} …", flush=True)
    if model_name == "gemma3_1b_it":
        from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
        tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("google/gemma-3-1b-it",
                                            local_files_only=True)
        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
            "google/gemma-3-1b-it", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
            local_files_only=True, attn_implementation="eager",
        ).to("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        if tok.pad_token is None:
            tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token
    else:
        model, tok = load_any_checkpoint(MODEL_PATHS[model_name])
    probe = MechInterpProbe(model, tok)
    print(f"[MI-5] {model_name}: moe={is_moe_model(model)} "
          f"layers={probe.n_layers} experts={probe.n_experts}", flush=True)
    return probe


def run_model_battery(model_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    empty_cache()
    gpu_determinism()
    probe = load_model(model_name)
    moe = is_moe_model(probe.model)

    battery: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "model": model_name,
        "path": MODEL_PATHS[model_name],
        "is_moe": moe,
        "n_layers": probe.n_layers,
        "n_experts": probe.n_experts,
    }

    # Paris-Auflösung je Modell (Plan-Risiko 4):
    paris_ids = resolve_paris(probe)
    battery["paris_id"] = paris_ids[0]
    battery["paris_id_is_argon_constant"] = bool(paris_ids[0] == 9079)

    # SP3-Felder (modell-agnostisch):
    battery["sp3"] = sp3_fields(probe)
    battery["sp3"]["paris_recall"] = bool(
        battery["sp3"]["paris_top1_id"] == battery["paris_id"])

    # Kristallisations-Trajektorien (alle 4, Logit-Lens modell-agnostisch):
    rows = subset_rows(probe)
    battery["layerwise_logits"] = {
        name: {
            "star": crystallization_start(
                [r for r in info["rows"] if r["kind"] == "layer"]),
            "rows": [{"layer": r["layer"], "kind": r["kind"],
                      "top1_id": r["top1_id"], "top1_str": r["top1_str"],
                      "top1_prob": r["top1_prob"], "gap_tv": r["gap_tv"]}
                     for r in info["rows"]],
            "official_top1_id": info["official_top1_id"],
            "official_top1_str": info["official_top1_str"],
            "p_final_top1_id": info["p_final_top1_id"],
            "anchor_tv": info["anchor_tv"],
        }
        for name, info in rows.items()
    }

    if moe:
        # MI-1-Tomographie je Subset-Text:
        battery["routing_pct"] = {
            name: probe.layerwise_routing([text])["pct_26x8"].tolist()
            for name, text in DOMAINS.items()}
        battery["bias_std_26"] = bias_std_26(probe.model)

        # V5-Felder (tokenwise H(ℓ11) prose/code):
        battery["ent_l11"] = ent_l11_series(probe)

        # MI-2-arm (ℓ17-Paris-Share):
        sub = probe.sublayer_logits(
            PARIS_TEXT, {"id": battery["paris_id"], "str": " Paris"})
        row17 = sub["rows"][17]
        denom = row17["dp_attn"] + row17["dp_moe"]
        battery["l17_paris"] = {
            "dp_attn": row17["dp_attn"], "dp_moe": row17["dp_moe"],
            "p_mid": row17["p_mid"], "p_out": row17["p_out"],
            "share_attn_17": float(row17["dp_attn"] / denom) if denom != 0 else 0.0,
            "anchor_tv": sub["anchor_tv"],
            "telescope_broken": sub["telescope_broken"],
        }

        # [Datums-Zusatz] M_digit-Serie unter Bias-Bedingungen:
        battery["m_digit_series"] = m_digit_series(probe)

        # [Datums-Zusatz] Chimären je Variante (MI-4-Kanon):
        battery["chimera_tokenwise"] = {
            "prose_code": chimera_tokenwise(probe, CHIMERA_PROSE_CODE,
                                            PREFIX_PROSE_CODE),
            "prose_math": chimera_tokenwise(probe, CHIMERA_PROSE_MATH,
                                            PREFIX_PROSE_MATH),
        }
    else:
        # [Datums-Zusatz] dense-arm (MI-2-Analog auf gemma):
        try:
            sub = probe.sublayer_logits_dense(
                PARIS_TEXT, {"id": battery["paris_id"], "str": " Paris"})
            row17 = sub["rows"][17]
            denom = row17["dp_attn"] + row17["dp_mlp"]
            battery["dense_paris"] = {
                "status": "gemessen",
                "dp_attn_17": row17["dp_attn"], "dp_mlp_17": row17["dp_mlp"],
                "p_mid_17": row17["p_mid"], "p_out_17": row17["p_out"],
                "share_attn_17": float(row17["dp_attn"] / denom) if denom != 0 else 0.0,
                "telescope_target": sub["telescope_target"],
                "endpoints_target": sub["endpoints_target"],
                "telescope_broken": sub["telescope_broken"],
                "anchor_tv": sub["anchor_tv"],
                "rows_dp": [{"layer": r["layer"], "dp_attn": r["dp_attn"],
                             "dp_mlp": r["dp_mlp"],
                             "top1_out_str": r["top1_out_str"]}
                            for r in sub["rows"]],
            }
        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as err:
            battery["dense_paris"] = {"status": "OFFEN/strukturell",
                                      "error": str(err)}

    return battery


def resolve_paris(probe: MechInterpProbe) -> List[int]:
    ids = resolve_token_ids_exact(probe.tokenizer, " Paris", (" Paris",))
    if len(ids) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "MI-5: Paris-Auflösung nicht unique "
            f"({ids}) — kein stiller Fallback.")
    return ids


# -- Strikte Auswertung nach PREREG (kein stiller Fallback) ------------------

def classify_battery(batteries: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    verdicts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    argon = {name: b for name, b in batteries.items()
             if name in ARGON_VARIANTS}
    base = argon["argon_1b"]

    # V1 — lineage routing shift:
    try:
        detail = {}
        for sibling in ("argon_1b_specialized", "argon_1b_specialized_ml"):
            d_s = [layer_mean_tv(
                torch.tensor(argon[sibling]["routing_pct"][dom]),
                torch.tensor(base["routing_pct"][dom]))
                for dom in DOMAINS]
            detail[sibling] = {"per_text": d_s, "mean": mean_value(d_s)}
        ok = all(d["mean"] > TV_FLOOR for d in detail.values())
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_lineage_routing_shift",
                         "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
                         "detail": detail, "floor": TV_FLOOR})
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_lineage_routing_shift", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    # V2 — paris_recall_compare (alle 4):
    try:
        rows = {name: {"top1": b["sp3"]["paris_top1_id"],
                       "resolved": b["paris_id"],
                       "ok": b["sp3"]["paris_recall"]}
                for name, b in batteries.items()}
        ok = all(r["ok"] for r in rows.values())
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_paris_recall_compare",
                         "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", "detail": rows})
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_paris_recall_compare", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    # V3 — dense_parent_argmax (gemma):
    try:
        g = batteries["gemma3_1b_it"]["sp3"]
        top1_str = g["generic_top1_str"]
        cond_a = top1_str in FORMAT_TOKENS
        cond_b = g["p_digit1_generic"] >= V3_P1_FLOOR
        cond_c = g["M_digit_generic"] >= V3_M_FLOOR
        ok = cond_a and cond_b and cond_c
        verdicts.append({
            "id": "mi5_dense_parent_argmax",
            "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
            "detail": {"top1_str": top1_str, "top1_id": g["generic_top1_id"],
                       "p_digit1": g["p_digit1_generic"],
                       "M_digit": g["M_digit_generic"],
                       "cond_a_format_token": cond_a,
                       "cond_b": cond_b, "cond_c": cond_c},
            "floors": {"p1": V3_P1_FLOOR, "M": V3_M_FLOOR},
        })
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_dense_parent_argmax", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    # V4 — crystallization_compare (alle 4):
    try:
        detail = {}
        for name, b in batteries.items():
            stars = {dom: info["star"]
                     for dom, info in b["layerwise_logits"].items()}
            defined = sum(1 for s in stars.values() if s is not None)
            detail[name] = {
                "star_by_domain": stars,
                "defined": defined,
                "of": len(b["layerwise_logits"]),
                "anchors": {dom: {"p_final_top1_id": info["p_final_top1_id"],
                                  "official_top1_id": info["official_top1_id"],
                                  "anchor_tv": info["anchor_tv"]}
                            for dom, info in b["layerwise_logits"].items()},
            }
        ok = all(d["defined"] >= V4_MIN_DEFINED for d in detail.values())
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_crystallization_compare",
                         "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
                         "detail": detail, "floor": V4_MIN_DEFINED})
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_crystallization_compare", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    # V5 — gate_entropy_gradient (alle 3 argon):
    try:
        detail = {}
        for name, b in argon.items():
            prose_h = b["ent_l11"]["prose"]["H_mean_l11"]
            code_h = b["ent_l11"]["code"]["H_mean_l11"]
            detail[name] = {"H_prose_l11": prose_h, "H_code_l11": code_h,
                            "gap_code_minus_prose": code_h - prose_h,
                            "gate_mean_l11_prose":
                                b["ent_l11"]["prose"]["gate_mean_l11"],
                            "gate_mean_l11_code":
                                b["ent_l11"]["code"]["gate_mean_l11"]}
        ok = all(d["gap_code_minus_prose"] > V5_GAP_FLOOR
                 for d in detail.values())
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_gate_entropy_gradient",
                         "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
                         "detail": detail, "floor": V5_GAP_FLOOR})
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_gate_entropy_gradient", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    # V6 — l17_attn_share_compare (alle 3 argon):
    try:
        detail = {name: {"share_attn_17": b["l17_paris"]["share_attn_17"],
                         "dp_attn": b["l17_paris"]["dp_attn"],
                         "dp_moe": b["l17_paris"]["dp_moe"]}
                  for name, b in argon.items()}
        ok = all(d["share_attn_17"] >= V6_SHARE_FLOOR
                 for d in detail.values())
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_l17_attn_share_compare",
                         "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
                         "detail": detail, "floor": V6_SHARE_FLOOR})
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as err:
        verdicts.append({"id": "mi5_l17_attn_share_compare", "status": "OFFEN",
                         "error": str(err)})

    return verdicts


def summarize(verdicts: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    n_pass = sum(1 for v in verdicts if v["status"] == "PASS")
    n_fail = sum(1 for v in verdicts if v["status"] == "FAIL")
    n_offen = sum(1 for v in verdicts if v["status"] == "OFFEN")
    return f"{n_pass} PASS | {n_fail} FAIL | {n_offen} OFFEN"


def classify(verdicts: List[Dict[str, Any]], pilot_fails: int, gpu: bool) -> int:
    if any(v["status"] == "FAIL" for v in verdicts):
        return 1
    if pilot_fails:
        return 2
    if gpu and any(v["status"] == "OFFEN" for v in verdicts):
        return 2
    if gpu and verdicts:
        return 0
    return 2


def main() -> int:
    gpu = "--gpu" in sys.argv
    json_path = GPU_DEFAULT_JSON
    if "--json" in sys.argv:
        json_path = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--json") + 1]

    pilot_rows = [{"id": rid, "status": "PILOT" if ok else "PILOT-FAIL",
                   "note": note}
                  for rid, ok, note in (p() for p in PILOTS)]
    pilot_fails = sum(1 for r in pilot_rows if r["status"] == "PILOT-FAIL")

    batteries: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    verdicts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    if gpu:
        for model_name in MODEL_PATHS:
            batteries[model_name] = run_model_battery(model_name)
            empty_cache()
        verdicts = classify_battery(batteries)
        payload = {"pilots": pilot_rows, "batteries": batteries,
                   "verdicts": verdicts}
        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(payload, fh, ensure_ascii=False)

    for row in pilot_rows:
        print(f"{row['status']}: {row['id']} — {row['note']}", flush=True)
    if gpu:
        for v in verdicts:
            print(f"{v['status']}: {v['id']}", flush=True)
        print(summarize(verdicts), flush=True)
        print(f"JSON: {json_path}", flush=True)

    print("\n=== SUMMARY ===", flush=True)
    pil = f"{len(pilot_rows) - pilot_fails}/{len(pilot_rows)} PILOT"
    if gpu:
        print(f"PILOT {pil} | VERDIKTE {summarize(verdicts)}", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"PILOT {pil} | BATTERIE OFFEN (CPU-Lane)", flush=True)

    return classify(verdicts, pilot_fails, gpu)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""scout_gemma_structure.py — MI-5 Instrument-Scout (S1/S2, KEINE Messung).

Prüft NUR Struktur-Fakten von google/gemma-3-1b-it lokal aus dem HF-Cache:
Layer-Attribut-Namen (moe? self_attn? mlp?), Kopf-Pfad-Module
(model.model.norm / model.lm_head / model.model.embed_tokens),
Hook-Ausgabeformen für den geplanten dense-arm (sublayer_logits_dense via
h_mid = h_in + attn_out, gemma3 pre-norm residual). Keine Top-1s, keine
Wahrscheinlichkeiten, keine digit-Massen — die Meßdati entstehen erst im
registrierten MI-5-Batterie-Lauf (P2: Registrierung vor Messung).

Run: python scratches/scout_gemma_structure.py     (GPU, venv)
"""

import json

import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

MODEL_ID = "google/gemma-3-1b-it"


def main() -> int:
    facts: dict = {"model_id": MODEL_ID}

    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_ID, local_files_only=True)
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL_ID, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, local_files_only=True,
        attn_implementation="eager",
    ).to("cuda")
    model.eval()

    facts["type"] = type(model).__name__
    facts["inner_type"] = type(model.model).__name__
    facts["vocab_size"] = int(model.config.vocab_size)
    facts["tie_word_embeddings"] = bool(model.config.tie_word_embeddings)
    for k in ("final_logit_softcapping", "attn_logit_softcapping"):
        facts[f"config_{k}"] = getattr(model.config, k, None)

    layer = model.model.layers[0]
    facts["n_layers"] = len(model.model.layers)
    facts["layer_attr"] = {
        name: hasattr(layer, name)
        for name in ("moe", "self_attn", "mlp", "input_layernorm",
                     "post_attention_layernorm", "pre_feedforward_layernorm",
                     "post_feedforward_layernorm")}
    facts["submodules"] = sorted(k for k, _ in layer.named_children())

    for name, mod in (("norm", model.model.norm),
                      ("lm_head", model.lm_head),
                      ("embed_tokens", model.model.embed_tokens)):
        facts[f"has_{name}"] = mod is not None
        facts[f"type_{name}"] = type(mod).__name__

    # Hook-Formen-Verifikation (kleiner Forward, nur Shapes/dtypes):
    h_in, attn_out, mlp_out, layer_out = [], [], [], []
    handle_in = layer.register_forward_pre_hook(
        lambda _m, args: h_in.append(args[0].detach()))
    handle_attn = layer.self_attn.register_forward_hook(
        lambda _m, _i, o: attn_out.append(
            (o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o).detach()))
    handle_mlp = layer.mlp.register_forward_hook(
        lambda _m, _i, o: mlp_out.append(
            (o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o).detach()))
    handle_out = layer.register_forward_hook(
        lambda _m, _i, o: layer_out.append(
            (o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o).detach()))
    try:
        ids = tok("Paris is the capital of", return_tensors="pt",
                  add_special_tokens=True).input_ids.to("cuda")
        with torch.no_grad():
            _ = model(ids)
    finally:
        for h in (handle_in, handle_attn, handle_mlp, handle_out):
            h.remove()

    def shape(t: torch.Tensor) -> str:
        return f"{tuple(t.shape)}/{t.dtype}"

    facts["hook_shapes"] = {
        "layer_input[0]": shape(h_in[0]),
        "self_attn_out[0]": shape(attn_out[0]),
        "mlp_out[0]": shape(mlp_out[0]),
        "layer_out[0]": shape(layer_out[0]),
        "n_captures": {"in": len(h_in), "attn": len(attn_out),
                       "mlp": len(mlp_out), "out": len(layer_out)},
    }
    facts["mid_shape_ok"] = bool(
        h_in[0].shape == attn_out[0].shape and
        h_in[0].dtype == attn_out[0].dtype and
        h_in[0].shape == layer_out[0].shape)

    print(json.dumps(facts, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

### bytes: 36503   lines: 781
